## AI. Section AH re-test after the fixes (D-508 to D-522)

Run these on the build with the section AH fixes (D-508 to D-522) on the owner's PC. Item 420 adds columns to item 397's logger that every later AI line reads; frame names stay exactly as item 397 defines them (F is the first row whose isPaused reads true for a hit you land, and it draws the contact pose; heldShown is true from F+1; W is the row the word's number first shows; E is the first row your isPaused reads true for an enemy blow on you; r and f are the capture panel's counts). Run item 420's checks first, then item 421 (shot 1). Report every pass line with its numbers. The table after item 437 says what became of every AH and AG line the PC failed on 0582a04, and lists the AG items still to run; item 438 is the rehearsal and the recording.

420. [ ] **The logger for section AI (D-510, D-514, D-519; adds to item 397, corrects 397(b)).** Item 397's logger at Enum.RenderPriority.Last.Value + 2, with its frame names and columns, plus these. Each later AI line names the column it reads.
   1. Frame time: the first statement of the Last+2 step reads os.clock(); the row's frame time is this read minus the last row's. Beside it, log the render step's dt (the step's argument) and r.
   2. The drawn body for rush lines: a second render step bound at Enum.RenderPriority.Input.Value + 1, before the rigs' step, logs HumanoidRootPart.CFrame * DrawnBody.offset(model) and the boar's Torso CFrame. That is the pose this row draws: the last step's pose on the root physics moved, drawn again after physics (D-514). The Last+2 read is the next row's pose against the root before physics (about drawn − velocity × dt): judge no rush step and no wall face on it. If the rush lines below fail on the Input+1 read by about item 401(c)'s error (−0.12 to −0.43 a row at 60 fps), the frame was drawn before PostSimulation and D-514's redraw changed nothing: report it.
   3. Physics: your root and each enemy root in a RunService.PreSimulation connection and in a PostSimulation connection of the same frame, with root.AssemblyLinearVelocity at PreSimulation.
   4. The server root (for items 422 and 423): a runtime-only server script logs, each Heartbeat from the boar's RushStarted to 1 s after its stop, the server clock, the boar root's position, AssemblyLinearVelocity, Humanoid.WalkSpeed and the told stop (RushEnded's contact for a slam, the line end for an open run). Mark three rows. land: the first Heartbeat whose WalkSpeed leaves the rush speed (28) for the landing pace (EnemyService's engine.land, once the flat distance left is ≤ 28 × min(dt, 1/30) + 0.001). landingUntil: land's clock plus the distance left on that row / 28. landed: the first Heartbeat at or after landingUntil, where the flat velocity reads 0 (engine.halt). Superseded by item 439.
   5. The sword's mount (D-519): character:GetAttribute("WeaponMount") ("Back" or "Hand") and character:GetAttribute("WeaponMountWhy") beside the Weapon motor's Part0 (the motor is character.Weapon.Weapon.Weapon). The logger's Drawn column is the server's combat tag; where this client hangs the blade is WeaponMount.
   6. Clip time and world tip: each row, the local rig's live clip id and clip time, and the weapon tip in world space, solve(Part0) * C0 * Transform * C1:Inverse() * TrailEnd (not Weapon.CFrame, which reads a frame stale). A row's tip step is |tip − the previous row's tip|.
   7. tipDrawn: the tip logged on the row before. The Last+2 read comes after this row's step has written the pose, which is drawn on the next row (D-342), so the logged tip runs one row ahead of the picture; tipDrawn is what this row draws.
   8. Shot 6's clocks (D-511): on the client, the go reply's receivedAt (DevStage stamps every reply), the Telegraph payload's serverTime, and workspace:GetServerTimeNow() on the row the Telegraph arrives.
   9. Gaps: judge only pairs of rows whose r values are consecutive. Report every r gap (the PC's relay drops batches of about 50 rows at 144 Hz) and any row whose dt is over twice the median; a jump across a gap or a stall is not a move.
   (a) Pass (397(b), corrected; D-510): setup A with Workspace DevCapture = true and workspace:SetAttribute("DevFrameCap", 30), one 10 s take. Every row's os.clock frame time is ≥ 33.30 ms; rows the engine did not overrun are 33.33–33.5 ms; rows over 34.3 ms are engine overruns (the work before the cap took longer than 33.3 ms), and the row after one is again ≥ 33.30 (the cap's releases are ≥ 33.333 ms apart and never catch up).
   (b) Report (397(b)): the dt column, not judged per row. Single rows can read 33.3 ± 2.5 ms (Lune 31.5–35.1; the PC 31.94–35.66 with 60 rows under 33.2), because dt carries the change in the work after the cap; its mean over any 30 rows is 33.3 ± 0.2 ms.
   (c) Pass: r rises 300 ± 3 over any 10 s (the PC 300).
   (d) Confirm: both frame-time columns side by side for the take. Expect no os.clock row under 33.30 where dt has some. If the os.clock column also has rows under 33.30, something that varies runs between Last+1 and Last+2: then also log os.clock at the end of the logger's step, and list what is bound at Last+1 to Last+2.

## AJ. Section AI re-test after the fixes, first part (D-523 to D-526)

Run these on the build with the first part of the section AI fixes (D-523 to D-526) on the owner's PC. Frame names stay exactly as item 397 defines them (a row is the logger's at Last+2), and item 420's columns stand except column 4, which item 439 replaces. Report every pass line with its numbers. The render check, measured on 4b18b5c at 60 fps with rows at Last+2: a joint Transform written on PostSimulation never shows on screen (the boar's Root lifted 3 studs and the avatar's RightShoulder turned +80°: neither visible); written in the render step at Camera−1 it shows, steadily; written on PreSimulation it shows on the boar, while on the avatar's arm the game's own PreSimulation write replaces it. On every test the screen matched BasePart.CFrame, and BasePart.CFrame at Last+2 equals the previous row's joint-chain read (300 of 301 rows): the screen shows the pose as physics solved it, so D-514's PostSimulation redraw has no visible effect. Items 422(a), 423(b) and 424 stay open, keep their AI lines and are being re-worked, and the fails in 430 to 437 are being fixed separately; a second part of this section follows with both.

439. [ ] **The server root log, corrected marks (D-525; replaces item 420 column 4).** A runtime-only server script logs, each Heartbeat from the boar's RushStarted until 1 s after its stop, the server clock, the boar root's position, AssemblyLinearVelocity, Humanoid.WalkSpeed and the told stop (RushEnded's contact for a slam, the line end for an open run). "along" is the root's flat distance from the launch point along the run; times are from RushStarted. Mark these rows.
   1. land: for a slam, the first Heartbeat whose flat distance to the told stop is ≤ 28/30 + 0.001 = 0.934 (the rush speed times ai.movement.haltLeadSeconds, 1/30) or whose WalkSpeed is under 28, whichever comes first; for an open run, the Done row (the run's end stays on its timer).
   2. The landing rows: from land on, WalkSpeed reads about the distance left / (2 × that row's dt) (ai.movement.landFrames 2), capped at 28 (at 144 Hz it reads 28 until the body is about 0.39 from the stop), and shrinks each row.
   3. landingUntil: land + 0.25 (ai.movement.landMaxSeconds). It is now a bound, not the landing's end.
   4. landed: the first Heartbeat after land whose flat velocity reads 0 (Engine.halt).
   5. The rest: along on the landed row, and along 1 s after the stop.

"Off" with tries 2 or more after "NotTaken" (or tries 2 with no row before): the engine dropped the first turn-off and the read-back caught it. 3. "Off" after "NoHumanoid" or "NotEnemy": the Humanoid or EntityKind arrived after the tag. 4. "StoodUp": the copy was already FallingDown when the client reached it (on that one row at most). 5. "Waiting" with tries climbing to 240: a client SetStateEnabled does not hold at all; log the why, and say whether the earlier probe's helper ran in the command bar or in a LocalScript. 6. ## AK. Section AI re-test after the fixes, second part (D-527 to D-529)

Run these on the build with the second part of the section AI fixes (D-527 to D-529) on the owner's PC, after section AJ. Frame names stay exactly as item 397 defines them (a row is the logger's at Last+2), and item 420's columns stand as item 439 left them. Judge every pose on the BasePart read at Last+2, which is what the screen shows (section AJ's intro); log the joint-chain read beside it for diagnosis. Report every pass line with its numbers. Section AI's lines that these replace are marked "Superseded by item …"; the rest of 433 to 437 stand.

444. [ ] **The sheathed guard's tip, presses judged only when sheathed (D-529; replaces 433(b)).** Setup C, 240 fps capture, sheathed, item 420 columns 1, 5, 6 and 7.
   1. Before each press wait until column 5 reads WeaponMount "Back" (about 7.2 s after the last guard release: the 6 s combat tag, sheatheDelay 0.3 and the Sheathe's 0.815 s), or space the presses 9 s apart.
   2. Each row, the screen tip in the root's frame: HumanoidRootPart.CFrame:PointToObjectSpace(the Weapon part's BasePart CFrame × TrailEnd), both read at Last+2; divide its step by the row's column-1 frame time. Log column 6's chain tip through the same root beside it.
   3. Count the PreSimulation callbacks between consecutive Last+2 rows (the row's physics substeps) and log the count.
   (a) Pass: on presses whose press row reads "Back", the screen tip is ≤ 84 studs/s on every row with exactly 1 substep (Lune 61.5 standing, 64.1 walking 16; the PC on 4b18b5c 301 at peak, 135 rows over 84, most of them on presses that were drawn). Superseded by item 475.
   (b) Report: rows with 0 or 2 substeps, apart, with their tip step. If a 0-substep row shows no tip step and the next row shows two rows' worth, the PC's 301 is frame pacing, not the clip.
   (c) Report: presses whose press row reads "Hand", apart. A drawn guard is now a snap (D-527): Lune 340–358 studs/s on 1 row at 60 Hz, 914–1070 on 4 rows at 240 Hz; not judged.
   (d) Report (433(c)): the first row column 5 reads "Hand" after a sheathed press, and WeaponMountWhy (Lune: at the Draw's WeaponDraw, 0.22 s after the press, about 53 rows at 240 fps; Why nil).
445. [ ] **The Draw's tip with the substep count (replaces the PC's 433(d) Draw reading).** Item 345's Draws, standing and at a sprint, 240 fps, with item 444's screen tip and substep count (444 steps 2 and 3).
   (a) Pass: ≤ 84 studs/s on rows with exactly 1 substep (Lune 73–75 at 240 Hz, 68.4 standing, ≤ 73.8 over a sprint's cycle; the PC on 4b18b5c 171 at peak, 63 rows over 84). Superseded by item 475.
   (b) Report: 0- and 2-substep rows apart, with the root's PreSimulation-to-PostSimulation move on that row (column 3; at 24 studs/s 0, 0.1 or 0.2 studs).
446. [ ] **The lock turn's pivot foot against the yaw physics applied (replaces 434(a)).** Setup C, lock a target 90°, 135° and 179° off each way, 60 fps. Superseded by item 476.
   1. Each row log the pivot foot's grounded corners from the foot's BasePart at Last+2 (the screen) and from the chain beside, and the root's yaw at PreSimulation before the lock write, after it (pendingLockYaw flushed), and at PostSimulation (column 3).
   (a) Pass: the pivot foot's least-moving grounded corner moves ≤ 0.01 a row on the BasePart read through the turn (Lune 0.003–0.005; the PC on 4b18b5c 0.097 at +90, 0.010–0.025 elsewhere).
   (b) Report if (a) fails: each row's yaw physics applied (PostSimulation minus PreSimulation after the write) minus the yawAhead the pose was built with. A gap that is not zero means the turn the body shows is not the turn the pose expected.
   (c) Report: the lifts (Lune and the PC: L2 and R1 on + turns, L1 and R2 on − turns).
447. [ ] **The drawn idle's feet (D-529; replaces 435(a)).** The drawn idle, 60 fps, 300 rows.
   (a) Pass: on the BasePart read, every foot's lowest corner is within ±0.02 of the floor on every row, except the drawn stance's rear (right) foot, which D-417 sets heel-up (Lune LeftFoot −0.003 to 0.000).
   (b) Pass (435(e), as written): the rear foot's ball ≤ 0.05 over the floor with its heel up (Lune ball +0.033 to +0.038, heel about 0.13; the PC 0.033–0.038).
448. [ ] **The drawn walk's plants (D-529; replaces 435(c)).** The drawn walk at 8, 60 and 144 fps, 10 s each. Superseded by item 477.
   (a) Pass: each foot is planted (a grounded corner within 0.02 and the least-moving corner ≤ 0.02 a row) on 55–65% of rows (Lune 60% and 60% at 60 fps, 61% and 59% at 144; the PC 58–61%).
   (b) Pass: at 60 fps the step p90 of the least-moving grounded corner is < 0.02 on the BasePart read (Lune 0.0187 and 0.0191; log the chain beside).
   (c) Report: at 144 fps the p90 (Lune's screen model 0.0504, chain 0.0200; the PC 0.050–0.052) with each row's PreSimulation count. Lune expects 1 or 2 substeps alternating (1 on about a third of the rows) and the large steps on the rows whose count differs from the row before. Not judged until the gait builds its pose for the frame's own physics travel (Open items in PROGRESS).
449. [ ] **The landing on the move at W 24 (D-529; replaces 435(d)).** The landing at W 24 from 0.25 s and 0.5 s falls, 60 and 144 fps.
   (a) Pass: each row's foot turn ≤ max(20, the sprint's own per-row foot turn) + 1° (Lune sprint's own 31.3 drawn, 31.1 sheathed; the PC's sprint 33–35.3 gives a bar of 34.3–36.3, so its 33.22 and 31.13 pass).
   (b) Report: the sprint's own foot turn over one cycle before the jump, on the same take.
450. [ ] **Out of a drawn sprint into an attack (D-529; replaces 436(a)).** Out of a drawn sprint at 24 into Basic1 and into Horizontal, 60 fps.
   (a) Pass: each row's foot turn ≤ the sprint's own per-row foot turn + 1° on the rows the sprint still shows (Lune feet 26.7 and 26.1 into Basic1, 26.4 and 24.9 into Horizontal; the sprint's own 31.3; the PC 26.9 and 26.0).
   (b) Report: the arms (Lune 21.2 and 20.9, up to 21.8; the PC under 20; still open).
451. [ ] **The drawn guard raise is a snap (D-527; replaces 436(c)).** The guard raised drawn, standing and walking at 8, with nothing to cut, 60 fps.
   (a) Pass: from the 4th row after the press (cancel.snapFrames 3) every upper-body part turns ≤ 8° a row (review.audit.reportDegrees) on the BasePart read (Lune after D-527: standing 89.3 / 8.9 / 0.9 / 0.1 / 0.1, walking 115.7 / 11.6 / 1.5 / 0.8 / 0.5; the PC on 4b18b5c 9.5 / 29.6 / 29.8 / 25.6 / 7.7).
   (b) Report: the first rows (the snap itself; not judged against 20°).
   (c) A guard pressed sheathed: item 444 instead (D-420's Draw over it, unchanged).
452. [ ] **A dodge takes the body without a pop (D-528; replaces 436(d)).** The four dodges from the drawn and the sheathed stance, standing, walking at 8 and running at 16 (stick held), 60 fps; mark the press row and the row State first reads Dodge. Superseded by item 478.
   (a) Pass: from the press row until the dodge clip lets go, every body part turns ≤ 20° a row on the BasePart read (Lune after D-528: arms ≤ 19.6, legs ≤ 18.9, torso and head ≤ 8.4; the PC on 4b18b5c 30.9–60.3 on the arms).
   (b) Report: the rows between the press and the Dodge state (Lune: the clip plays from the press row).
   (c) Report: the dodge reaches its pose by 0.18 s (arms) and 0.12 s (legs, spine and root); its move and i-frames are unchanged. A dodge out of a basic is a cancel (the snap, D-408): report it apart.

## AL. Section AI re-test after the fixes, third part (D-530 to D-533)

Run these on the build with the third part of the section AI fixes (D-530 to D-533) on the owner's PC, after section AK. Rows are the logger's at Last+2 and every pose is judged on the BasePart read (the screen, section AJ's intro), with the joint-chain read logged beside it for diagnosis. The rush's Torso is now drawn off its root joint on this client while the rush is away from its root (D-531), so the joint chain no longer shows the body during a rush: read the Torso, Snout and feet BasePart CFrames. If item 453 fails, set choreo.luau enemy.rush.detachBody = false, rebuild, report the failure and run 454 to 458 again on that build (the body then draws through the joint as on 4b18b5c). Section AI's lines that these replace are marked "Superseded by item …"; 423(d) and the rest of 422 and 424 stand. The server root still rests short of or past its stop at 60 fps until section AJ's landing fails are fixed; the snout bar below is measured beyond the root's own rest.

453. [ ] **The detached Torso, engine checks (D-531).** Play with Server and Client, setup C, while a FrenzyBoar charges (S("charge", …) in the open, then into item 401's wall), at 60 fps.
   1. Each row on the client log the boar's Torso.Root.Enabled, Torso.Anchored, the Humanoid's GetState() and the Torso BasePart position; on the server log the same two properties each Heartbeat.
   (a) Pass: on the client Enabled reads false and Anchored true from the first row the rush is drawn away from its root (Run) until Done, and true and false on every row after; on the server they read true and false on every row (the client's writes never replicate). Superseded by item 482.
   (b) Pass: on the row the Torso goes back on its joint there is no fling or pop: the Torso step is ≤ 28 × dt + 0.05 (item 456) and the Humanoid's client state is the same as the row before. Superseded by item 482.
   (c) Report: whether the Head, Snout and legs follow the drawn Torso on the same row or one row late (the clip pose lagging the Torso by a row is the assumed worst case).
454. [ ] **The wall runs on the screen (D-531; replaces 423(a) and (b)).** Item 423's slam at the wall: 60 fps at lag 0, 0.05 and 0.25 s, and 144 fps at lag 0, 3 takes each (the PC's x423 runs). Per row the Snout BasePart's distance past the wall face, the drawn step of the Torso BasePart, and the server root (item 439).
   (a) Pass: while the boar is drawn away from its root the snout is ≤ 0.05 past the face on every row, the late root's catch-up row included (Lune replays of the PC's x423 rows 0.000; the PC on 4b18b5c 0.372 / 0.367 / 1.607 / 0.027). At rest it may sit past the face by no more than the server root's own rest past the told stop (item 440).
   (b) Pass: every drawn step ≤ 28 × dt + 0.05, dt that row's own (Lune ≤ 0.467 at 60 and ≤ 0.194 at 144 on every replay; the PC up to 1.248 over the run's travel), and no backward step over 0.02 in the 10 rows after the stop. Superseded by item 485.
455. [ ] **The rush's Torso step on the screen (D-530; replaces 422(a)).** Shot 6 at lag 0 and 0.05 s, 3 takes each, and item 454's runs. Per row the Torso BasePart's step and the drawn root's step (DrawnBody.position's step).
   (a) Pass: on every row not crossing the Charge clip's launch snap the Torso steps ≤ 0.6 at 60 fps (Lune ≤ 0.484; ≤ 0.208 at 144).
   (b) Pass: on the launch row and the 2 rows after it the Torso steps ≤ the drawn root's step + 0.42 (Lune 0.737–0.853 = 0.466 + the clip's 0.308–0.431; the PC's chain 0.758–0.834).
   (c) Pass: run, skid and slam rows ≤ the drawn root's step + 0.12 (Lune ≤ + 0.109); crouch rows ≤ + 0.2 (Lune + 0.173). Superseded by item 486.
456. [ ] **The hand-back's end (D-531).** Items 454 and 455's takes. Mark the row Torso.Root.Enabled first reads true again.
   (a) Pass: the Torso BasePart's step on that row and the row after is ≤ 28 × dt + 0.05 (Lune: the root's own move in that row, 0 on every PC run because the root is at rest by then). Superseded by item 482.
457. [ ] **The nameplate over a charging boar (D-533).** Items 454 and 455's takes. Per row the nameplate BillboardGui's Adornee, its StudsOffsetWorldSpace, and the plate's place (the Adornee's BasePart position + StudsOffsetWorldSpace) against the drawn body (DrawnBody.position) plus the plate's lift.
   (a) Pass: the Adornee is the Torso on every row Torso.Root.Enabled reads false, and the root part again from the row it reads true. Superseded by item 483.
   (b) Pass: the plate's place is within 0.05 of the drawn body plus its lift on every detached row (Lune 0.000; before the fix 0.491 / 0.487 / 0.487 at 60 fps and 0.216 at 144, the root's predicted move), and by eye it shows no lag or jump on the run, the stop or the re-attach. Superseded by item 484.
   (c) Report: the SWITCH prompt over a charging boar, the same way, if one shows.
458. [ ] **Two charges, the slope and the first launch step on the screen (D-531; replaces the reads in 424(a), (c) and (d)).** Item 424's setups and takes as written, read on the Torso and Snout BasePart instead of at Input+1.
   (a) Pass (424(a)): every drawn step of the second charge ≤ 28 × dt + 0.05, the first-move row included. Superseded by item 485.
   (b) Pass (424(c)): the uphill charge ends Done at 22.00; every horizontal step ≤ 28 × dt + 0.05 and every 3D step ≤ horizontal / cos 40° + 0.05 (Lune 0.609 at 60 fps). Superseded by items 485 and 487.
   (c) Pass (424(d)): the first launch step ≤ 28 × dt + 0.01, dt that row's own. Superseded by item 485.
   (d) 424(b) as written.

## AM. Section AI re-test after the fixes, fourth part (D-534 to D-537)

Run these on the build with the fourth part of the section AI fixes (D-534 to D-537) on the owner's PC, after section AL. Rows are the logger's at Last+2 and every pose is judged on the BasePart read (the screen, section AJ's intro), with the joint-chain read logged beside it for diagnosis. First and Character+1 are item 397's frame names; Character+1 is the root right after stepStandOff.

459. [ ] **Every hit's number shows, counted two ways (D-536; replaces 430(c)).** Setup D, shot 4 (Sharp Nail) at the 30 fps cap, lag 0 and 0.05 s; then at 60 and 144 Hz, and shots 2, 3 and 5.
   1. Count a number on each row where any DigitAnchor part's CFrame changes (only Digits.show writes it), or its DigitGui TextLabel's Text changes, or its TextTransparency drops from above 0.9 to 0. Log each HitConfirmed's row and the anchor written on it.
   (a) Pass: numbers equal the counted hits at every rate: Sharp Nail 3, Horizontal 1, Vertical 1, Rising Arc 2, the kill 1.
   (b) Pass: the distinct DigitAnchor parts lit also equal the hits: 3 anchors for Sharp Nail's 3 hits at 30 fps (before D-536 the 3rd hit, 0.600–0.637 s after the 1st, took the 1st's freed anchor, so a per-anchor count read 2).
460. [ ] **The code's step at the kobold, split (corrects 430(d)).** Setup D, shots 2, 4 and 5 at 30, 60 and 144.
   1. Bind a logger at Enum.RenderPriority.Character.Value + 2 and read the root there, and at First. The code's step on row N is the root at Character+1 of N minus the root at First of N; split it along the flat line to the kobold into "toward" and "back or aside".
   (a) Pass: back or aside ≤ 12 × dt on every row (Lune max: Sharp Nail 0.183 at 60, 0.280 at 30, 0.082 at 144; Horizontal and Rising Arc 0). Superseded by item 500.
   (b) Report "toward" apart: it is the clip's own lunge (Lune peaks at 60 Hz: Horizontal 0.400 at clip 0.367; Sharp Nail 0.285 at 0.350 and 0.400 at 0.867; Rising Arc 0.285 at 0.350).
   (c) Report the engine's share apart: First of N+1 minus Character+1 of N. (The Last+2 root equals Character+1's in position; the PreSimulation writes change only the lock's yaw.)
461. [ ] **Hands at the kobold's walk-in rests, as the screen draws them (D-535, D-537; replaces 430(e)).** Setup A, the walk-in rests at 60 and 144 Hz, each move. Superseded by item 498.
   (a) Pass: on every row no hand or arm reads more than 0.05 inside the kobold's body parts or its Weapon on the BasePart read (Lune's drawn read at the PC's rests 3.15–3.60, square and turned 10.35°: ≤ 0.006 in the body; the PC on 4b18b5c: Horizontal 0.090 at 60 and 0.057 at 144, the chain's Basic3 right hand 0.19 at 60 and 0.260 at 144). Log the joint-chain read beside it (Lune ≤ 0.008).
   (b) On each swing's first row log WeaponMount (D-537 plans a sheathed swing with the sword in the hand) and the flat root-to-root distance.
   (c) Pass: the same read at 30 fps (Lune ≤ 0.008).
   (d) Report by eye: the body stands up to 0.20 further back at Rising Arc's first key at 60 Hz (0.30 at 30 fps) and every cut still lands (Open items in PROGRESS).
   (e) 409(d) as written.
462. [ ] **The stand-off at a flush wall and pillar (D-534; replaces 431(a), (b) and (d)).** Setup C, item 431's takes as written (the wall, then the pillar, flush behind; a held kobold in front; Basic1, then Sharp Nail 3 times), at 30, 60 and 144. Superseded by item 496.
   (a) Pass: the code's share of the root's move toward the kobold over Basic1 (the sum over its rows of Character+1 minus First) is ≤ 0.001 (Lune −0.004 at the wall and at the pillar). Report the engine's share apart, First of N+1 minus Character+1 of N (Lune about 0.32 over Basic1 from a flush start: 0.06 a row at 60, 0.115 at 30; the PC on 4b18b5c +0.090 and +0.047).
   (b) Pass: on any row where a colliding part is within 0.05 of the face or in it, the code's step back is 0.000 (Lune 0.000 at 30, 60 and 144; before D-534 0.125 / 0.116 / 0.149; the PC up to 0.095 on 875 rows).
   (c) 431(c) as written.
   (d) Pass: the code's step is ≤ 12 × dt (Lune max 0.200 at 60, 0.083 at 144; the PC 4 and 3 rows over, 0.284 and 0.268). The UpperTorso offset check uses the BasePart read at Last+2 against the clip pose at the previous row's clip time.
   (e) 431(e) as written.
463. [ ] **Basic3's step back against the in-game plan (D-537; replaces 436(e) and (f)).** Setup C, a held kobold 3.5 studs in front, three presses (Basic3 plays only as the chain's third, so 436(e) folds into this item).
   1. On Basic3's first row log the flat root-to-root distance d, then run in the Client command bar at that row's staging (k is the held kobold under workspace.Enemies):
   `local P=game:GetService("Players").LocalPlayer; local RS=game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage"); local CP=require(P.PlayerScripts.Client.controllers.ContactPredictor); local clip=require(RS.Shared.clips.player.OneHandedSword.Basic3); local me=P.Character; local k=<the held kobold>; local r=me.HumanoidRootPart.Position; local kp=k.HumanoidRootPart.Position; local plan=CP.standOff(me, clip, 0, CFrame.lookAt(r, Vector3.new(kp.X, r.Y, kp.Z)), k, CP.worldCast(me), {keepOut=true, step=false}); print(plan ~= nil, plan and plan.keep and plan.keep(0.10), plan and plan.keep and plan.keep(0.15), plan and plan.keepSide and plan.keepSide(0.15))`
   (a) Pass: the root's back step at clip 0.10 equals the plan's keep(0.10) within 0.05 (the PC on 4b18b5c: 0.598 / 0.549 / 0.400 at d 3.20 / 3.25 / 3.35, against plans 0.55 / 0.55 / 0.45).
   (b) Pass: from 0.10 the root's raw net move back is keep(t) minus the clip's own forward root motion (0.50 by 0.15, 0.60 by 0.25), within 0.05.
   (c) Pass: with the sword on the back (WeaponMount "Back" on that row) the probe prints the same plan as with it in the hand (before D-537 the sheathed plan was 0 at 3.2–3.75).
   (d) Report: the kobold's facing (its yaw off the line to your root) and its HeldBlocker part's offset from your root. The PC's aside (0 for Basic1, 0.0287 for Basic3) is where Lune's square-on kobold reads 0.166 / 0.228 (Open items).
   (e) Repeat with Basic1 (one press; the probe with clips.player.OneHandedSword.Basic1), read the same way.
   (f) 436(g) as written.
464. [ ] **Basic1's blade out of the kobold after the cut (D-534, D-535, D-537; replaces 437(e)).** Setup C, a held kobold at 3.5, Basic1, drawn and sheathed. Superseded by item 499.
   1. On Basic1's first row log WeaponMount and the lock or assist target (TargetController's lock id; DevAim "status").
   (a) Pass: the blade (the Weapon BasePart at Last+2) is out of the kobold by the row at clip time 0.283 and stays out, whether that row read Back or Hand (Lune's screen read: out by 0.283; the chain beside it 0.25 +0.21, 0.267 −0.01, 0.283 −0.13).
   (b) Pass: at the key (0.15) the blade is ≤ 0.25 deep (the plan caps the reach at 0.2 + 0.005; Lune 0.19–0.21 with the PC's own 0.45 keep).
   (c) Report: the root's back step at Character+1 over clip 0 to 0.15 (Lune keep 1.05 back by 0.133; the PC's −0.47 at 0.15 on 4b18b5c meant no keep-out was shown).

## AN. AG re-test after the ribbon fixes (D-538 and D-539)

Run these on the build with the ribbon fixes (D-538, D-539) on the owner's PC, after section AM. Setup for every item: Playground, Test > Clients and Servers with 2 players; A swings and B watches, B's window in front at 60 fps (DevFrameCap where a rate is named). Item 397's logger at Last+2 with item 406's TrailLit and TrailOwner columns and item 415's Weapon motor Part0 column; positions from BasePart reads (the tip is Weapon.CFrame * TrailEnd.CFrame, the hand RightHand.CFrame). Rows: P is the first row whose Part0 reads RightHand; T the first row whose TrailLit reads true for A's rig; N the row that shows the stroke's end pose, as 382(e) reads it. B's Incoming Replication Lag is set to 0, 0.1 and 0.25 s in turn.

465. [ ] **A late remote basic's ribbon (D-538; replaces 376(a)).** A's Basic1, from sheathed and again already drawn, 4 takes per lag.
   (a) Lag 0, as before: T is 5 rows after P (0.084 s), the ribbon about 5.4 studs, its first point at least 5 studs from row P−1's tip. Superseded by item 493.
   (b) Pass at lag 0.1 (Studio's actual catch-up is about 0.134): at least 1 ribbon segment on rows T to T+3 in 4 of 4 takes; the first point within 1.5 studs of row T's tip and at least 5 studs from row P−1's tip; no segment longer than 1.5 studs and none after row N. Expect very short ribbons (Lune at 60 fps, catch-up 0.10 / 0.11 / 0.12 / 0.134: 2.48 / 1.49 / 0.66 / 0.06 studs sheathed, 3.90 / 2.62 / 1.21 / 0.06 drawn); a take with no visible ribbon at a catch-up of 0.13 or more is the rule working, not a fail. Superseded by item 481.
   (c) Pass at lag 0.25: no segment, and TrailLit never true for the swing (the window closed at 0.24).
466. [ ] **The sword in the hand a frame early (measurement only; replaces 376(b)).** Sheathed takes at lags 0, 0.1 and 0.25, on B (remote) and on A's own screen (local). Superseded by item 490.
   (a) Report: row P's tip jump (P−1 to P) and the hand's move on P, and the hand's move on P+1. Expected now: on P the tip jumps 4.10–4.14 studs while the hand moves ≤ 0.003; on P+1 the hand moves 0.03–0.15 (Lune 0.038 at lag 0, 0.133 at catch-up 0.25). The fix is approved and comes with a later section; its pass line will be: on row P the hand moves at least 0.03, and no row shows the sword in an unmoved hand.
467. [ ] **No ribbon after the end pose, on A's own screen (D-539; replaces 383(a)).** Vertical at DevFrameCap 30, 60 and 144; 3 takes each: into the boar, into a kobold, into the air. Count ribbon segments on every row.
   (a) Pass: no new segment on any row after N, the row after the last paused row and the settle included; the last new segment is on row N (the PC on 4b18b5c: 2 after the hit-stop at 30, 4 in the settle at 144; Lune before 1 at 30 and 60, 2 at 144; now 0).
   (b) 382(a) to (d) as written. Superseded by item 492.
468. [ ] **No ribbon after the end pose, on a remote rig (D-539; replaces 384(a)).** On B, A's Vertical at 60, 30 and 144 fps, lags 0 and 0.1, 2 takes each.
   (a) Pass: item 467's (a) on B's rows (the PC on 4b18b5c: 4 right after the end pose at 60). Superseded by item 491.
   (b) 382(a) to (d) as written. Superseded by item 492.
469. [ ] **A faster remote clip under lag (corrects 374(b); no code change).** A's Horizontal at speed 1.0 and 1.3 (item 374's beginArt call), B's lag 0 and then 0.25.
   (a) First read B's lag-0 offset from the lag-0 takes (the PC about +0.019 / +0.020). Then d = (the 1.0 take's lateness at lag 0.25) − that offset.
   (b) Pass: the 1.3 take at lag 0.25 runs ((0.25 + d) × 1.3 − 0.25) / 1.3 + the offset behind, ± 0.017 (one 60 fps frame). With the PC's 4b18b5c numbers d = 0.014, expected 0.091, read 0.106: a pass. The old line's 0.058 assumed the message came exactly 0.25 s late. Superseded by item 494.

## AO. Re-test after the enemy clip, upright, landing, ribbon and measure fixes (D-540 to D-550)

Run these on the build with the enemy clip, upright, landing, ribbon and measure fixes (D-540 to D-550) on the owner's PC, after section AN. Rows are item 397's logger rows at Last+2. In the ClipLab a row is named by the clip time it logs; in game, row 0 is the first row where the enemy's root moved more than 0.001 from the row before. At 60 Hz 0.15 s is row 9 and 0.5 s row 30; at 144 Hz rows 22 and 72. A bar of 0.1 a 60 Hz frame is 0.042 a 144 Hz row.

470. [ ] **The boar's parry stagger rears (D-540; replaces 391(c)).** ClipLab (item 8), Boar, at 60 Hz then on a 144 Hz monitor, at 1x and 0.25x: play enemy/Boar/Stagger and log each hoof's lowest corner and the clip time each row. In game, setup C: perfect-parry a boar's blow and log the same from the first row of its Stagger. Superseded by item 510.
   (a) Pass: at clip time 0.10–0.20 s the front hooves read 0.95–1.10 (Lune front left 1.05–1.08, front right 0.96–0.98; the PC 0.96–1.02); 144 Hz reads the same range.
   (b) Pass: both front hooves read ≤ 0.02 by the 0.42 s row (Lune −0.013 from 0.40), and one footstep plays within a row of 0.42 s.
   (c) Pass: the back hooves stay between −0.02 and 0.02 and move ≤ 0.02 over the clip (Lune −0.012; the PC moved 0.001–0.003).
471. ## AP. Re-test after the boar rush hand-over and sword-to-hand fixes (D-551 to D-555)

Run these on the build with the boar rush hand-over and sword-to-hand fixes (D-551 to D-555) on the owner's PC, after section AO. Rows are item 397's logger rows at Last+2 unless a line names PreSimulation or PostSimulation, and every pose is judged on the BasePart read (the screen), with the joint-chain read logged beside it for diagnosis. "The drawn root" is DrawnBody.position, which on this build answers where the step drew the body while the Torso is drawn off its joint (D-552); read it through the game's own module from the client's command bar or the logger. dt is the row's own frame time. Items 482 to 489 re-test section AL's rush lines (setup C, Server and Client, a FrenzyBoar charging as section AL's items name); item 490 re-tests item 466 with section AN's setup. The lines these replace are marked "Superseded by item …"; the rest of AL and AN stands.

On each row log Torso.Root.Enabled, Torso.Anchored, the Torso BasePart and the joint chain at Last+2, and the Torso BasePart at PreSimulation (after the game's handler) and at PostSimulation. The hand-back's last row is the first row whose DrawnBody.offset reads nil.
   (a) Pass: on the client Enabled reads false and Anchored true from the first row the rush is drawn away from its root through the hand-back's last row, and true / false from the next row on; on the server true / false on every row.
   (b) Pass: on the hand-back's last row the Torso BasePart is within 0.05 of the chain read (Lune 0.000; f446653 0.627 to 0.641).
   (c) Pass: every Torso BasePart step from 3 rows before the row Enabled first reads true to 6 rows after it is ≤ 28 × dt + 0.05 (Lune ≤ 0.034 at 60 fps, ≤ 0.011 at 144, ≤ 0.052 at 30; f446653 0.640 to 0.699 at 60), and the Humanoid's client state is the same as the row before.
   (d) Report: the PreSimulation read just after the hand-over, which may be stale by about the P4 probe's 0.036 to 0.049 (never drawn), and the PostSimulation read on that row (on its chain).

   (a) Pass: the plate's place is within 0.05 of DrawnBody.position + the plate's lift on every row of the run, the stop, the stagger and the hand-back (Lune ≤ 0.001; f446653 0.459 to 0.521 at 60 fps). Superseded by item 505.

   (a) Pass: every drawn-root step is ≤ 28 × dt + 0.05, the launch rows and the uphill run included (Lune ≤ 28 × dt), and the first launch step of the drawn root is ≤ 28 × dt + 0.01.

"The drawn root" is DrawnBody.position, read through the game's own module, as in section AP. R is the first row whose kobold root Anchored reads false; M is the first row from R whose root (BasePart at Last+2) has moved more than 0.001 from R−1's; H, the arrival row, is the first row after M whose root reads Anchored true again. Build the rescue place as on 82b82d4: copy the repo, never edit the tracked file; in the copy set src/shared/data/ai.luau upright.client.catchOnArrival = false; build and play from the copy, and confirm at run time that catchOnArrival reads false, resumeOnRelease true and resumeSettleSteps 12; afterwards close without saving, delete the copy and check git status is clean.
   1. Rescue build: S("pack", {enemy = "KoboldAxeman", position = {80, 0, -20}, count = 1}) and hold it at once with S("hold", {ids = {koboldId}}), as on 82b82d4 (P1 #8, 443 #38); then S("enemy", {entityId = koboldId, position = {80, 0, -20}, facing = 0, hold = true}), so it stands at (80, 0, −20) looking along −Z. Log about 200 held rows.

   5. Rescue build at 144 fps: repeat actions 1 and 2.

## AT. Re-test after the enemy walk start, turn and stop, boar breath, sheathed lock-turn and Brute room fixes (D-618 to D-629)

Run these on the build with the enemy walk start fixes (D-618, D-621, D-625), the enemy walking-turn plant and the boar walk's re-keyed first frame (D-619, D-620), the boar's breath (D-622), the enemy stop plant (D-623), the sheathed idle's weight shift (D-626) and the Brute's back room (D-627 to D-629) on the owner's PC, after section AS. D-624 changes ClipAudit's Lab plan only and nothing on screen. Rows are item 397's logger rows at Last+2, and every pose is judged on the BasePart read (the screen). A joint-chain read asked for here is a report beside it, for diagnosis: it reads every joint's Transform. First and Character+1 are item 397's frame names; Character+1 is the root right after stepStandOff. Rates: 30 is DevFrameCap 30, 60 the in-game menu's Default and 144 the menu's 144 FPS; dt is the row's own frame time. Server rows are Heartbeat rows of item 439's server log. "97cea9d" is section AS's build, before these fixes. Item 532 re-tests section AR's 511 (the enemy walk start, now with its turn and its stop); item 533 adds new lines to AR's 510 (the boar's Stagger blend-out and its breathing idle), whose (a) to (d) stand; item 534 is new (the sheathed twin of item 529); item 535 re-tests section AS's 519(g) (a Brute that walks the fighter back to the world); item 536 is a diagnostic for 518(f) (a walking kobold drawn in steps on the PC's client), and item 537 takes 520(e)'s θS line on B, which section AS could not log. The lines these replace are marked "Superseded by item …"; the rest of AR and AS stands.

   1. Sheathe takes with the Skid, 5 at each rate: DevAim "draw" while sprinting. Let go of W 0.0, 0.1, 0.25, 0.4 and 0.6 s after the Sheathe's first row, the first row whose logged Sheathe handle weight is above 0. Key the release to that row, not to the command.
   2. Draw takes with the Skid, 5 at each rate: sprinting sheathed, DevAim "draw". Let go of W 0.1, 0.25, 0.4, 0.5 and 0.6 s after the Draw's first row, the first row whose logged Draw handle weight is above 0. On 97cea9d a release timed from the command landed 0.50–1.05 s into the Draw.
   3. Stop takes with no Skid, 4 at each rate, with the Sheathe and then with the Draw: run at 16 (no sprint) and let go of W 0.0, 0.1, 0.25 and 0.4 s after the clip's first row (the two-step stop). Where a gamepad is at hand, add 528 step 3's eased-stick takes.
   4. Log each row at Last+2: os.clock; the dt argument the Camera-1 render step got; the number of PreSimulation callbacks since the last row, and each one's deltaTime; Weapon.CFrame × TrailEnd.CFrame and Weapon.CFrame × TrailStart.CFrame, both in the root's frame (root:PointToObjectSpace); the Weapon motor's Part0; the Skid, Sheathe and Draw handle weights and the Sheathe or Draw clip time; and the root's flat speed. Also log os.clock in a PostSimulation connection, for (g). Keep every row: buffer the rows and save them after the take. A take that loses rows inside the clip's window is re-run, not judged (97cea9d lost 40–80 rows in 9 of 18 windows at 240).
   5. The tip's speed is item 475's rule with clock(N) row N's os.clock at Last+2: for each row N with at least one PreSimulation callback since row N−1, M is the last earlier row with at least one, and the speed is |tip(N) − tip(M)| / (clock(N−1) − clock(M−1)). Do not use PostSimulation clocks: a row's last PostSimulation is the frame before's physics, carried over a frame that ran none, so at 240 fps a step over such a frame reads twice its speed.
   6. The blade's line on a row is the unit vector from TrailStart to TrailEnd in the root's frame; its turn from row M to row N is the angle between the two lines, over step 5's clock span.
   (a) Pass: on every row of the Sheathe or the Draw in steps 1 and 2, the tip's speed is ≤ 84 studs/s. Run these on the build with the locked walk's step take-up (D-651), the enemy attack's wait for its drawn body (D-652), ClipLab's walk pace and play once (D-653) and the gait under a dodge aimed at the stick (D-657) on the owner's PC, after section AU. D-654, D-655 and D-656 correct lines and change nothing on screen. Rows are item 397's logger rows at Last+2, and every pose is judged on the BasePart read (the screen). A joint-chain read asked for here is a report beside it, for diagnosis: it reads every joint's Transform. First and Character+1 are item 397's frame names; Character+1 is the root right after stepStandOff. Rates: 30 is DevFrameCap 30, 60 the in-game menu's Default and 144 the menu's 144 FPS; dt is the row's own frame time. Server rows are Heartbeat rows of item 439's server log. The logger posts its rows batched, one post per file per second. "aa27e35" is section AU's build and "3eee415" section AT's, both before these fixes. Item 555 re-tests section AS's 530(a), the take-up's read, with 477(a) beside it; item 556 re-tests AU's 540(b) to (d) and AT's 532(k) with the attack waiting for its drawn body; items 557 and 558 re-test AT's 532(a), (d), (e) and (j) and AU's 540(a) on the PC's drawn rows; items 559 and 560 re-test AT's 532(m) and 533(a) in ClipLab. Item 561 re-tests AU's 548(d) and (e) and AT's 535(f), each row judged on its own step (D-655) and the engine's share read per hold and per swing (D-656). Item 562 re-tests AU's 550 step 4 and (d) with the gait aimed at the stick under a dodge, at 60 and 144 fps. The lines these replace are marked "Superseded by item …"; the rest of AS, AT and AU stands.

Run these on the build with the Brute's stop cast along the line (D-659) and landed in the open field (D-660), the take-up held through the engine's flips (D-661) and the local walk drawn on an even root (D-675), an enemy's attack waiting with no origin heard (D-663), an enemy's body drawn on an even root (D-664) with the boar's turn on the spot (D-665) and the Charge's lifted crouch (D-666), the keep-out's lead over its whole budget (D-667), a late stroke's back-fill on the body the rig draws (D-670), the stance kept out of the whole hold (D-671), the aside sharing a lunge row's budget (D-672) and an unlocked press's stance at the soft assist's pick (D-673, D-674) on the owner's PC, after sections AU and AV. D-658, D-662, D-668 and D-669 correct lines and change nothing on screen. Rows are item 397's logger rows at Last+2, and every pose is judged on the BasePart read (the screen). First and Character+1 are item 397's frame names; Character+1 is the root right after stepStandOff. Rates: 60 is the in-game menu's Default and 144 the menu's 144 FPS; dt is the row's own frame time. "d310ca7" is section AV's build and "aa27e35" section AU's, both before these fixes. Item 563 re-tests AU's 538 step 2 and (b) to (d) on the Head's hull; item 564 re-tests AU's 548(a) and (b) and AV's 561(a), (b), (e) and (g); item 565 re-tests AV's 555 step 2 and its (a), (c) and (d) on the walk's even root; items 566 to 568 re-test AV's 556, 557(a) to (d) and 558 on the even drawn body; item 569 is new (an enemy's drawn body under its hits, its outline and its shatter); items 570 to 572 re-test AU's 551(a), 542(d), 552(g), 553(b) and 553(g); item 573 re-tests AU's 539(a) to (c), and item 574 AV's 561(c) and (f).
